Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any black_holes module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 849. The projective resolution has length at most 439 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 849 + O(1/n^439). Summing over all modes and applying the black_holes convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on black_holes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 433. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 750 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to black_holes theory, we decompose the problem into 433 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 750. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
